# 1.32.0 Release

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1917) · state: closed · merged: True

## Original PR description

# cuDNN Frontend v1.32.0 Release Notes

cuDNN Frontend v1.32.0 adds Mamba-2, Qwen sparse attention training, Rubin triangle operations, and a CuTe DSL normalization backend. FROST SDPA gains more native forward and backward configurations, broader paged-cache support, and lower setup overhead. This release also adds FROST plan serialization, expands JAX grouped GEMM support, and updates Python bindings and dependencies.

## New: Mamba-2 linear attention 🚀 🚀

Mamba-2 joins the FROST linear-attention family with forward, backward, and state-summary operations on SM100, SM103, and SM107. The Python entry point is `cudnn.linear_attention.ops.mamba2` ([#1577](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1577)).

- Supports packed sequences, BF16/FP16 inputs, initial and final recurrent states, optional skip connections, output gating, and step-size controls. The supported state dimension is 128.
- State summaries expose the per-sequence end state and transition needed to compose sequence segments. Summary backward is also available.
- Checkpoints can use `checkpoint_dtype=torch.float32`; Mamba-2 checkpoint intervals are multiples of 128 tokens ([#1694](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1694)).

The wider linear-attention family also gains persistent caching for common forward kernels, a faster KDA backward pipeline, and fixes for multistream workspace reuse, GDP interleaved layouts and cached summaries, and GDN-2 NaNs under steep per-channel decay ([#1708](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1708), [#1699](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1699), [#1569](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1569), [#1664](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1664), [#1695](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1695), [#1644](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1644)).

## New: Qwen sparse attention training 🚀 🚀

`cudnn.ops.qsa_forward` and `qsa_backward` provide explicit packed QSA training calls for Blackwell SM100/SM103 and Rubin SM107. They support BF16 D256 attention with 12Q/1KV or 24Q/2KV heads and four-token blocks. Forward returns the BF16 output, an FP32 saved output, and natural-log LSE for backward. These functions do not install an autograd wrapper ([#1765](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1765), [#1778](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1778)).

- **Indexed training** accepts existing token selections through `qsa_indexed_forward` and `qsa_indexed_backward`, initially for BF16 12Q/1KV D256 on SM100/SM103. At 128K tokens with selection width 2052, compact IDs use 256.5 MiB in either direction, compared with 16 GiB for bitmap-forward IDs or 8 GiB for paired bitmap-backward IDs. The indexed path also avoids the 4 GiB selection bitmap. These figures describe selection metadata, not total memory ([#1774](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1774)).
- **Packed causal top-k backward** is available through `selection_mode="causal_topk"`, with explicit sequence boundaries and a bounded-workspace two-pass implementation. This mode has a stricter selection contract and requires the CUDA 13 CuTe DSL compiler ([#1781](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1781), [#1787](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1787)).
- QSA attention and selection functions are consolidated under `cudnn.ops.qsa_*`. Indexed attention consumes caller-supplied selections; the separate selector APIs compute them.

## Gated attention block: serving and packed training 🚀 🚀

The experimental Rubin SM107 D256 gated attention block gains paged-cache **EXTEND** and **DECODE** modes. These combine projection, optional QK normalization, RoPE, cache writes, attention, gating, and output projection through the block API. Weight loaders cover the documented Hugging Face and vLLM layouts ([#1471](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1471), [#1606](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1606), [#1619](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1619)).

- **Qwen sparse attention serving:** `QsaSpec` selects the index-list sparse attention path. Supported modes include caller-supplied lists and an integrated compression, scoring, and top-k indexer, including paged decode ([#1639](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1639), [#1789](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1789)).
- **Packed quantized execution:** the unfused MXFP8 forward and per-tensor FP8 backward gain packed-sequence support. Packed half forward can fuse the output gate, and supported packed backward plans accept an externally computed delta ([#1502](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1502), [#1505](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1505), [#1617](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1617), [#1497](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1497)).
- **Fewer launches:** MXFP8 backward fuses small operations and reuses block-scale dQ work. Serving paths add small-row projection kernels, fused preparation/cache writes, and programmatic dependent launch where supported ([#1466](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1466), [#1680](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1680), [#1763](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1763)).
- Compiled blocks can update quantization scales between steps through `update_quant_scales`. The fully fused MXFP8 projection also accepts MXFP4 weights ([#1506](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1506), [#1487](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1487)).

## SDPA: wider native forward support 🚀 🚀

**Blackwell SM100/SM103**

- Adds native FP16/BF16 **Q/K576, V512** attention with dense queries and dense or paged KV, including absorbed-MLA cache layouts. It supports partial GQA packing, split-KV, independent K/V tables and layouts, masks, sinks, and optional Stats. Packed queries remain outside this new flavor ([#1902](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1902)).
- Adds native paged per-tensor **FP8 D256** execution and physical-origin support for paged FP8 THD inputs. Paged FP8 attention can use sinks ([#1887](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1887), [#1895](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1895), [#1874](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1874)).
- Expands packed GQA prefill and D256 THD split coverage. Paged K and V can use independent physical layouts ([#1468](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1468), [#1533](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1533), [#1697](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1697)).

**Rubin SM107**

- Extends MXFP8 to packed D256 queries and paged K/V pools with sinks for supported dense D128/D256 queries ([#1488](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1488), [#1481](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1481)).
- Adds native D64 per-tensor FP8 attention, D128 GQA packing, and D512 packed/split plans. Half-precision sinks also work with split-KV ([#1878](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1878), [#1830](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1830), [#1863](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1863), [#1858](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1858)).
- MXFP8 and per-tensor FP8 forward rows are offered by default. The selected engine remains dependent on graph features and measured shape ranges ([#1478](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1478), [#1684](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1684)).

**SM120 and Ampere**

- SM120 gains specialized MXFP8 prefill kernels, including D512, plus FP16/BF16 decode tiles for small packed query counts. Split-KV supports attention sinks, and GQA packing no longer requires the full head group to divide the query tile ([#1599](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1599), [#1636](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1636), [#1768](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1768), [#1731](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1731), [#1740](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1740)).
- SM80 forward serves native packed THD graphs. Its backward reads the current ragged offsets on the device ([#1155](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1155), [#1058](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1058)).

Across the native THD paths, metadata setup is fused for small batches, tensor-map construction is parallelized, and setup overlaps kernel initialization. Context-parallel work lookup and paged scheduling use work prefixes and request lengths to reduce wasted work ([#1831](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1831), [#1851](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1851), [#1886](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1886), [#1696](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1696), [#1709](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1709)).

## SDPA: backward and numerical fixes 🚀 🚀

- **D128 backward on SM100/SM103:** adds FROST dense and packed execution, with supported GQA/MQA paths. Dense BF16/FP16 MHA can request a deterministic, fixed-order recompute implementation with zero workspace; grouped and THD deterministic requests remain unsupported ([#1688](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1688), [#1783](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1783), [#1875](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1875)).
- **More dense geometries:** adds native D64 backward and left sliding windows for dense D64/D128 MHA, plus native dense D192×128 half-precision backward on SM100 ([#1879](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1879), [#1883](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1883)).
- **D256 addressing:** supports batch-pitched backward and packed inputs with independent physical origins. Context-parallel backward reduces workspace and improves vector loads ([#1690](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1690), [#1872](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1872), [#1629](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1629)).
- Fixes negative attention-scale handling on supported SM100/SM107 and SM120 forward paths, masked leading KV tiles on Rubin, and D192 FP8 probability rounding ([#1445](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1445), [#1448](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1448), [#1544](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1544), [#1810](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1810)).
- Fixes D512 shared-ring phase reuse, split-KV softmax barrier ownership, scheduler back-edges, and terminal dQ barrier reuse ([#1521](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1521), [#1541](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1541), [#1547](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1547), [#1698](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1698)).

## New: Rubin triangle operations 🚀 🚀

`cudnn.triangle_attention` and `cudnn.triangle_multiplicative_update` provide experimental forward operations with cuEquivariance 0.12.0-compatible argument signatures ([#1607](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1607)).

- Triangle attention supports BF16/FP16 activations, pair bias, key masks or key lengths, and head dimensions divisible by 8 up to 64. Rows with no live keys return zero output and negative-infinity LSE.
- Triangle multiplicative update supports BF16 pair representations with channel widths 128 or 256, both directions, and optional pair masks.
- Both target **SM107 only** and require the documented FROST opt-in. Backward is not implemented.

## New: CuTe DSL normalization backend 🚀 🚀

SM100 gains forward and backward kernels for **LayerNorm, RMSNorm, GroupNorm, InstanceNorm, and BatchNorm**, with FP32, FP16, and BF16 I/O. They are available through graph engines and the direct `cudnn.norm.norm_fprop` / `norm_bprop` APIs ([#1509](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1509)).

GroupNorm, InstanceNorm, and BatchNorm have native channels-last paths that preserve layout through forward and backward. LayerNorm and RMSNorm reduce the last logical dimension and use the contiguous path for channels-last inputs.

Graph normalization APIs also accept optional RMSNorm scale, optional LayerNorm scale/bias, and optional AdaLayerNorm bias. RMSNorm and LayerNorm backward accept optional scale; LayerNorm validates matching statistics dimensions ([#1542](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1542), [#1548](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1548), [#1555](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1555), [#1552](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1552)).

## Sparse attention: DSA, BSA, and CSA ✨✨

- **Deterministic DSA:** adds deterministic indexer backward and standalone top-k, H32 indexer backward, and two-CTA H128 backward. A clustered reduction bounds workspace for long-context deterministic backward, including 1M-context workloads ([#1641](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1641), [#1637](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1637), [#1723](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1723), [#1666](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1666), [#1770](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1770)).
- **DSA indexer and execution:** adds MXFP4 scoring on SM100, native H96 target recomputation, and faster scale packing. Prepared DSA execution uses caller-owned outputs/workspace and preserves 64-bit addressing for long sequences ([#1775](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1775), [#1859](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1859), [#1564](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1564), [#1182](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1182), [#1767](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1767)).
- **Sage FP8 BSA:** adds native SM120 block-128 forward, SM90 block-64/block-128 forward, and SM100/SM103 block-128 forward. SM100 backward also supports padded KV blocks ([#1163](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1163), [#1752](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1752), [#1785](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1785)).
- **BSA correctness:** fixes SM90 shared-V lifetime, SM100 softmax stability for large logits, backward tail zero-fill, and FP32 computation of O·dO products ([#1498](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1498), [#1221](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1221), [#1682](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1682), [#1713](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1713)).
- **CSA:** adds deterministic dAPE reduction and parallelizes it. Ratio-128 forward handles NaN chunks and negative-infinity positions explicitly ([#1605](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1605), [#1717](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1717), [#1692](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1692)).

## GEMM, MoE, and JAX ✨✨

- **Canonical JAX grouped operations** expand across MXFP8, NVFP4/MXFP4, and BF16 wherever the underlying kernel supports the format. Coverage includes GLU, dGLU, weight gradients, quantization, SReLU/dSReLU, and Hadamard fusions. Packed FP4 travels as `uint8` with two values per byte ([#1399](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1399), [#1535](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1535), [#1600](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1600)).
- Grouped BF16, dGLU, gradient quantization, weight-gradient, and Hadamard paths accept canonical layouts. BF16 grouped execution no longer reads offsets back to the host ([#1422](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1422), [#1601](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1601), [#1602](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1602)).
- FROST GEMM gains SM120-specific tile scoring and split-K selection. The SM120 family adds MoE COMBINE and mixed MXFP4-weight/MXFP8-activation execution; callers can also own scheduler-counter initialization ([#1460](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1460), [#1500](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1500), [#1638](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1638), [#1881](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1881)).
- Fixes compiled-object cache reuse across CUDA devices in grouped GLU, Hadamard, and quantization. Also fixes the SM100 GLU/Hadamard/quant epilogue deadlock ([#1808](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1808), [#1813](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1813), [#1904](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1904), [#1901](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1901)).

## Graph API, serialization, and execution ✨✨

- **FROST plan serialization:** `pygraph.serialize()` records the selected Python engine, public knobs, graph IR, and optionally compiled kernels. Deserialization reuses kernels only when their recorded environment matches; otherwise it recompiles. `enforce_precompiled=True` rejects compilation. Python-plan blobs cannot be loaded by C++ `Graph::deserialize` ([#1862](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1862)).
- **Binary serialization:** `serialize(as_bytes=True)` returns `bytes`, and `deserialize()` accepts bytes. The default serialized return type remains `list[int]` ([#1725](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1725)).
- **CUDA Graph controls:** opt-in `populate_cuda_graph_direct()` / `update_cuda_graph_direct()` avoid an extra child graph for eligible plans. Applications that manage resource lifetimes can disable automatic retention with `cuda_graph_retention=NONE`; the default retains resources. Cleanup moves out of execution and into plan/graph destruction ([#1140](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1140), [#1495](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1495), [#1676](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1676)).
- **Shared compilation:** concurrent processes using the same compiled cache avoid duplicate compilation. SDPA plans also cache immutable function metadata ([#1707](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1707), [#1844](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1844)).
- **PyTorch integration:** `cudnn.torch.install_sdpa_overrides()` returns a removable handle for the cuDNN SDPA overrides, supporting PyTorch's Python-provider switch ([#1776](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1776)).
- **Unified SDPA compute precision** is forwarded to the backend with cuDNN 9.29 header/runtime gates. Explicit indexed execution remains independent of the selected plan, and legacy autotuning keeps metadata aligned with reordered plans ([#1746](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1746), [#1786](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1786), [#1806](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1806), [#1701](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1701)).

## Compatibility and API changes ✨✨

- **CuTe DSL minimum:** the package now requires `nvidia-cutlass-dsl[cu13]>=4.8.0`. Windows ARM64 remains excluded from this dependency and `apache-tvm-ffi`, because those runtimes do not provide packages for that platform ([#1820](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1820), [#1837](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1837)).
- **Bindings:** Python bindings move from pybind11 to nanobind. Builds require CMake 3.26 or newer. Optional `CUDNN_FRONTEND_PYTHON_STABLE_ABI=ON` targets GIL-enabled CPython 3.12+; free-threaded Python continues to require version-specific builds ([#1523](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1523)).
- **Experimental aliases removed:** explicit KDA/BSA operations move to `cudnn.ops`, and grouped SwiGLU aliases are removed. Differentiable JAX KDA/BSA entry points remain in `cudnn.jax` ([#1727](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1727), [#1597](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1597)).

| Removed name | Replacement |
|---|---|
| `cudnn.jax.kimi_delta_attention_fwd` / `_bwd` | `cudnn.ops.kimi_delta_attention_forward_jax` / `kimi_delta_attention_backward_jax` |
| `cudnn.jax.block_sparse_attention_forward` / `_backward` | `cudnn.ops.block_sparse_attention_forward_jax` / `block_sparse_attention_backward_jax` |
| `cudnn.torch.block_sparse_attention_forward` / `_backward` / `_fp8_forward` | Corresponding `cudnn.ops.block_sparse_attention_*` functions |
| `cudnn.jax.grouped_gemm_swiglu` or `cudnn.torch.grouped_gemm_swiglu` | `cudnn.grouped_gemm_swiglu_wrapper_sm100` |
| `cudnn.jax.grouped_gemm_dswiglu` or `cudnn.torch.grouped_gemm_dswiglu` | `cudnn.grouped_gemm_dswiglu_wrapper_sm100` |

- **`Amax_S` is deprecated.** FROST FP8 plans do not produce this output; a materialized `Amax_S` requires a backend plan ([#1684](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1684)).
- **Windows:** adds CUDA 13 dynamic loading, correct runtime-major dispatch for changed CUDA entry points, and Windows ARM64 packaging fixes ([#1499](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1499), [#1566](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1566), [#1714](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1714)).

## Documentation, benchmarks, and tests 📊

- Redesigned documentation navigation and indexed the lazy public API. FlashInfer-facing capability queries and runtime helpers are now public ([#1587](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1587), [#1663](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1663), [#1592](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1592)).
- Attention benchmarks now include the default frontend engine choice, a report of selection cost versus the fastest measured plan, context-parallel THD training, and serving-style paged KV with mixed cache lengths ([#1848](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1848), [#1856](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1856), [#1867](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1867)).
- Added QSA training benchmarks, normalization benchmarks, and Mamba-2 examples and measurements. Expanded CUDA Graph replay, deterministic execution, long-sequence, and multi-device regression coverage.
- `cudnn-repro` handles malformed tensor-dump lines and missing log files, and restores Python 3.9 import compatibility for that standalone tool ([#1584](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1584), [#1582](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1582)).
